			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			event_target:gsm_cur = { save_event_target_as = gsm_chart_src }
			gsm_gen_chart_build = yes
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = gsm_ui.11
	title = GSM_TICKER
	desc = gsm_ui.11.desc
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "gsm_win_company_fin"
	event_message_type = GSM_MESSAGE_TERMINAL
	picture_event_data = { room = no_video_feed_room }
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		gsm_ensure_exchange = yes
		gsm_ui_init_viewer = yes
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_ui_msg VALUE = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg_d value = gsm_ui_msg }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ex }
		gsm_ui_load_cur = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 0 }
			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_hqa value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_su_left value = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } event_target:gsm_cur = { is_variable_set = gsm_ind NOT = { has_country_flag = gsm_neutral } NOT = { is_country_type = gsm_group } NOT = { has_country_flag = gsm_defunct } } }
				event_target:gsm_cur = { gsm_gen_company_unitk = yes }
				set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_hqa value = event_target:gsm_cur.gsm_t_unitk }
				multiply_variable = { which = gsm_ui_hqa value = @gsm_hq_tax_free }
				round_variable_to_closest = { which = gsm_ui_hqa value = 0.1 }
				if = { limit = { event_target:gsm_cur = { is_variable_set = gsm_age check_variable = { which = gsm_age value < @gsm_startup_months } NOT = { has_country_flag = gsm_su_no } } } set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_su_left value = @gsm_startup_months } subtract_variable = { which = gsm_ui_su_left value = event_target:gsm_cur.gsm_age } }
			}
		}
	}

			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }

				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			gsm_gen_ui_assets = yes
			gsm_gen_ui_branch_deal = yes
		}

country_event = {
	id = gsm_ui.13
	title = GSM_TICKER
	desc = gsm_ui.13.desc
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "gsm_win_company_owners"
	event_message_type = GSM_MESSAGE_TERMINAL
	picture_event_data = { room = no_video_feed_room }
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		gsm_ensure_exchange = yes
		gsm_ui_init_viewer = yes
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_ui_msg VALUE = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg_d value = gsm_ui_msg }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ex }
		gsm_ui_load_cur = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 0 }
			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			gsm_gen_ui_owners = yes
		}
	}

		name = GSM_NAV_MARKET_BACK

				}
			}

			gsm_ui_build_ma = yes

		gsm_ensure_exchange = yes
		gsm_ui_init_viewer = yes

		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg_d value = gsm_ui_msg }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ex }
		gsm_ui_load_cur = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 0 }
			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			gsm_ui_build_cfd = yes
		}

country_event = {
	id = gsm_ui.18
	title = GSM_TICKER
	desc = gsm_ui.18.desc
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "gsm_win_group"
	event_message_type = GSM_MESSAGE_TERMINAL
	picture_event_data = { room = no_video_feed_room }
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		gsm_ensure_exchange = yes
		gsm_ui_init_viewer = yes
		gsm_ui_group_defaults = yes
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_ui_msg VALUE = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg_d value = gsm_ui_msg }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ex }
		gsm_ui_load_cur = yes
		gsm_gen_chart_clear = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 0 }
			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			event_target:gsm_cur = { save_event_target_as = gsm_chart_src }
			gsm_gen_chart_build = yes
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			gsm_ui_build_group = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = GSM_NAV_MARKET_BACK
		custom_gui = "gsm_o1"
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_back value = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_page value = 0 }
			country_event = { id = gsm_ui.1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = GSM_NAV_MARKET
		custom_gui = "gsm_o163"
		trigger = { NAND = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_back value = 1 } } }

	id = gsm_ui.19
	title = GSM_TICKER
	desc = gsm_ui.19.desc
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "gsm_win_group_owners"
	event_message_type = GSM_MESSAGE_TERMINAL
	picture_event_data = { room = no_video_feed_room }
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		gsm_ensure_exchange = yes
		gsm_ui_init_viewer = yes
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_ui_msg VALUE = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg_d value = gsm_ui_msg }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ex }
		gsm_ui_load_cur = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 0 }
			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			gsm_gen_ui_owners = yes
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = gsm_ui.25
	title = GSM_TICKER
	desc = gsm_ui.25.desc
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "gsm_win_group_actions"
	event_message_type = GSM_MESSAGE_TERMINAL
	picture_event_data = { room = no_video_feed_room }
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		gsm_ensure_exchange = yes
		gsm_ui_init_viewer = yes
		gsm_ui_group_defaults = yes
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_ui_msg VALUE = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg_d value = gsm_ui_msg }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ex }
		gsm_ui_load_cur = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 0 }
			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			gsm_ui_build_group_actions = yes
			gsm_gen_ui_gadd_rows = yes
			gsm_gen_ui_ginj_pick = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_ctl value = 1 } }
				gsm_ui_g_skip_held = yes
				event_target:gsm_cur = { set_variable = { which = gsm_t_gsind value = root.gsm_ui_g_ind } set_variable = { which = gsm_t_gsk value = 2000 } gsm_group_sub_quote = yes }
				set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_sok value = event_target:gsm_cur.gsm_t_gsok }
				set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_swhy value = event_target:gsm_cur.gsm_t_gswhy }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = GSM_NAV_MARKET_BACK
		custom_gui = "gsm_o1"
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_back value = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }

			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_page value = 0 }

country_event = {
	id = gsm_ui.27
	title = GSM_TICKER
	desc = gsm_ui.27.desc
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "gsm_win_group_rename"
	event_message_type = GSM_MESSAGE_TERMINAL
	picture_event_data = { room = no_video_feed_room }
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		gsm_ensure_exchange = yes
		gsm_ui_init_viewer = yes
		gsm_ui_group_defaults = yes
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_ctl value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_grp exists = event_target:gsm_ren_co@root event_target:gsm_ren_co@root = { gsm_is_live_group = yes } }
			if = { limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_sel@root } clear_global_event_target = gsm_sel@root }
			event_target:gsm_ren_co@root = { save_global_event_target_as = gsm_sel@root }
		}
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_grn_lost value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_grp } gsm_ui_rename_check = yes set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_grn_lost value = gsm_ui_ren_lost } }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_grn_gone value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_grp }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { exists = event_target:gsm_ren_co@root } }
				set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_grn_gone value = 1 }
			}
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { event_target:gsm_ren_co@root = { gsm_is_live_group = yes } } } set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_grn_gone value = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_grn_gone value = 1 } } gsm_ui_rename_end = yes set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 39 } }
		}
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_grn_busy value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_active NOT = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_grp } } set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_grn_busy value = 1 } }
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_gnm_p VALUE = 0 }
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_gnm_taken VALUE = 0 }
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_t_gnm_prev VALUE = 0 }
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_t_gnm_from VALUE = 0 }
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_grn_np VALUE = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_co save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_pl }
		if = { limit = { exists = capital_scope } capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_pl } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_active exists = event_target:gsm_ren_pl@root } event_target:gsm_ren_pl@root = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_pl } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_active exists = event_target:gsm_ren_co@root } event_target:gsm_ren_co@root = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_co } }
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_ui_msg VALUE = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg_d value = gsm_ui_msg }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ex }
		gsm_ui_load_cur = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 0 }
			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			if = { limit = { event_target:gsm_cur = { gsm_is_live_group = yes } } set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_g_ctl value = gsm_ui_is_ctrl } }
			gsm_ui_gnm_load_rename = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = GSM_NAV_MARKET_BACK
		custom_gui = "gsm_o1"
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_back value = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_page value = 0 }

country_event = {
	id = gsm_ui.15
	title = GSM_TICKER
	desc = gsm_ui.15.desc
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "gsm_win_company_rename"
	event_message_type = GSM_MESSAGE_TERMINAL
	picture_event_data = { room = no_video_feed_room }
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		gsm_ensure_exchange = yes
		gsm_ui_init_viewer = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_active exists = event_target:gsm_ren_co@root event_target:gsm_ren_co@root = { gsm_is_live_company = yes } }
			if = { limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_sel@root } clear_global_event_target = gsm_sel@root }
			event_target:gsm_ren_co@root = { save_global_event_target_as = gsm_sel@root }
		}
		gsm_ui_rename_check = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_ren_lost value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 34 } }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_co save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_pl }
		if = { limit = { exists = capital_scope } capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_pl } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_active exists = event_target:gsm_ren_pl@root } event_target:gsm_ren_pl@root = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_pl } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = gsm_ren_active exists = event_target:gsm_ren_co@root } event_target:gsm_ren_co@root = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ren_co } }
		gsm_default = { VAR = gsm_ui_msg VALUE = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg_d value = gsm_ui_msg }
		set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_msg value = 0 }
		event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_ex }
		gsm_ui_load_cur = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_cur value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 0 }
			event_target:gsm_exchange = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
			event_target:gsm_cur = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_has_home value = 1 } }
					event_target:gsm_t_home = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_home }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_home value = 1 } }
				}
				root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:gsm_hq@this }
					event_target:gsm_hq@this = { save_event_target_as = gsm_cur_hq }
					root = { set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_has_hq value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			gsm_ui_build_quotes = yes
			gsm_ui_nm_load_rename = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = GSM_NAV_MARKET_BACK
		custom_gui = "gsm_o1"
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = gsm_ui_back value = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }

			set_variable = { which = gsm_ui_page value = 0 }
